function saveNewPatient() {
  const name = document.getElementById('np_name').value.trim();
  const ward = document.getElementById('np_ward').value.trim();
  const bed = document.getElementById('np_bed').value.trim();
  if (!name || !ward || !bed) {
    showToast("Name, Ward, and Bed are mandatory");
    return;
  }

  const newPatient = {
    id: Date.now(),
    name,
    age: +document.getElementById('np_age').value || 45,
    gender: document.getElementById('np_gen').value,
    ward,
    bed,
    adm: Date.now(),
    dev: document.getElementById('np_dev').value.trim(),
    status: 'checked',
    on: true,
    base: +document.getElementById('np_base').value || 72,
    condition: document.getElementById('np_cond').value.trim() || 'General Admission',
    doctor: U.name,
    nurse: ''
  };

  S.p.push(newPatient);
  cur = newPatient.id;
  pushVital(newPatient.id, 'hr', newPatient.base, 'manual');
  pushVital(newPatient.id, 'spo2', 98, 'manual');
  pushVital(newPatient.id, 'temp', 36.8, 'manual');

  logAction(`Admitted new patient ${name} to Ward ${ward}/Bed ${bed}`, "Clinical");
  saveState();
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(`Patient ${name} added successfully`);
}

  modal(`

      <label for="mv_hr">${t('vitals_hr')} (bpm)</label>

      <label for="mv_spo2">${t('vitals_spo2')} (%)</label>
      <input id="mv_spo2" type="number" placeholder="e.g. 97">

      <label for="mv_temp">${t('vitals_temp')} (\u00B0C)</label>
      <input id="mv_temp" type="number" step="0.1" placeholder="e.g. 37.0">

      <button class="pri" onclick="saveManualVitals(${pid})">${t('btn_save')}</button>
      <button onclick="closeModal()">${t('btn_cancel')}</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveManualVitals(pid) {
  const hr = document.getElementById('mv_hr').value;
  const spo2 = document.getElementById('mv_spo2').value;
  const temp = document.getElementById('mv_temp').value;

  if (hr) pushVital(pid, 'hr', hr, 'manual');
  if (spo2) pushVital(pid, 'spo2', spo2, 'manual');
  if (temp) pushVital(pid, 'temp', temp, 'manual');

  const p = getPatient(pid);
  evaluatePatient(p);
  logAction(`Manual vital spot-check logged for ${p.name}`, "Clinical");
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(t('btn_save'));
}

function openQuickVitalModal(defaultPid) {
  const pid = defaultPid || cur || (S.p[0] && S.p[0].id);
  modal(`
    <div class="fm">
      <h2>${t('modal_quick_vital_title')}</h2>
      
      <label for="qvm_pid">${t('th_patient')}</label>
      <select id="qvm_pid">
        ${S.p.map(p => `<option value="${p.id}" ${p.id === pid ? 'selected' : ''}>${p.name} \u00B7 Ward ${p.ward}/Bed ${p.bed}</option>`).join('')}
      </select>

      <label for="qvm_hr">${t('vitals_hr')} (bpm)</label>
      <input id="qvm_hr" type="number" placeholder="e.g. 78">

      <label for="qvm_spo2">${t('vitals_spo2')} (%)</label>
      <input id="qvm_spo2" type="number" placeholder="e.g. 98">

      <label for="qvm_temp">${t('vitals_temp')} (\u00B0C)</label>
      <input id="qvm_temp" type="number" step="0.1" placeholder="e.g. 36.8">

      <button class="pri" onclick="submitQuickVitalModal()">${t('btn_save')}</button>
      <button onclick="closeModal()">${t('btn_cancel')}</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function submitQuickVitalModal() {
  const pid = +document.getElementById('qvm_pid').value;
  const hr = document.getElementById('qvm_hr').value;
  const spo2 = document.getElementById('qvm_spo2').value;
  const temp = document.getElementById('qvm_temp').value;

  if (!hr && !spo2 && !temp) {
    showToast("Please enter at least one vital parameter");
    return;
  }

  if (hr) pushVital(pid, 'hr', hr, 'manual');
  if (spo2) pushVital(pid, 'spo2', spo2, 'manual');
  if (temp) pushVital(pid, 'temp', temp, 'manual');

  const p = getPatient(pid);
  evaluatePatient(p);
  logAction(`Quick bedside vitals entered for patient #${pid} (${p ? p.name : ''}) by ${U ? U.name : 'Staff'}`, "Clinical");
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(`${t('btn_save')}: ${p ? p.name : ''}`);
}

function openBaselineModal(pid) {
  const p = getPatient(pid);
  modal(`
    <div class="fm">
      <h2>${t('modal_baseline_title')} ${p.name}</h2>
      <p class="mut">${t('modal_baseline_desc')}</p>
      
      <label for="base_hr">${t('lbl_baseline_hr')}</label>
      <input id="base_hr" type="number" value="${p.base || 72}">

      <button class="pri" onclick="saveBaseline(${pid})">${t('btn_set_baseline')}</button>
      <button style="margin-top:10px" onclick="clearBaseline(${pid})">Reset to Default Standard</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function saveBaseline(pid) {
  const val = +document.getElementById('base_hr').value;
  if (!val || val < 40 || val > 160) {
    showToast("Enter a realistic baseline heart rate (40-160)");
    return;
  }
  const p = getPatient(pid);
  p.base = val;
  evaluatePatient(p);
  logAction(`Baseline heart rate for ${p.name} set to ${val} bpm`, "Clinical");
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(`Baseline updated to ${val} bpm`);
}

function clearBaseline(pid) {
  const p = getPatient(pid);
  p.base = null;
  evaluatePatient(p);
  logAction(`Baseline heart rate cleared for ${p.name}`, "Clinical");
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast("Baseline reset to standard range");
}

function openAcknowledgeModal(alertId) {
  const alert = S.al.find(a => a.id === alertId);
  if (!alert) return;

  modal(`
    <div class="fm">
      <h2>${t('modal_ack_alert_title')}</h2>
      <p class="mut">Alert: <b>${alert.cat}</b> \u2014 ${alert.desc}</p>
      
      <label for="ack_note">${t('lbl_resolution_note')}</label>
      <textarea id="ack_note" rows="3" placeholder="e.g. Bedside assessment completed; oxygen administered at 2L/min..."></textarea>

      <button class="pri" onclick="confirmAcknowledgeAlert(${alertId})">${t('btn_confirm_resolve')}</button>
      <button onclick="closeModal()">${t('btn_cancel')}</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function confirmAcknowledgeAlert(alertId) {
  const alert = S.al.find(a => a.id === alertId);
  if (!alert) return;
  const note = document.getElementById('ack_note').value.trim() || 'Bedside evaluation conducted';

  alert.res = {
    t: Date.now(),
    by: U.name,
    note
  };

  logAction(`Alert resolved for patient #${alert.pid} by ${U.name}: ${note}`, "Alert");
  saveState();
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(t('status_checked'));
}

function openReviewConcernModal(concernId) {
  const c = S.concerns.find(x => x.id === concernId);
  if (!c) return;

  modal(`
    <div class="fm">
      <h2>${t('modal_review_concern_title')}</h2>
      <p class="mut">Submitted by: <b>${c.attender}</b><br>Message: "${c.text}"</p>

      <label for="rev_note">${t('lbl_staff_note')}</label>
      <textarea id="rev_note" rows="3" placeholder="e.g. Checked on patient; warm blanket provided and temperature rechecked..."></textarea>

      <button class="pri" onclick="confirmReviewConcern(${concernId})">${t('btn_acknowledge')}</button>
      <button onclick="closeModal()">${t('btn_cancel')}</button>
    </div>
  `);
}

function confirmReviewConcern(concernId) {
  const c = S.concerns.find(x => x.id === concernId);
  if (!c) return;
  const note = document.getElementById('rev_note').value.trim() || 'Attended bedside';

  c.status = 'reviewed';
  c.reviewedBy = U.name;
  c.reviewNote = note;

  logAction(`Attender concern #${concernId} reviewed by ${U.name}`, "Clinical");
  saveState();
  closeModal();
  draw();
  showToast(t('status_checked'));
}

function submitAttenderConcern(pid) {
  const name = document.getElementById('att_name').value.trim() || 'Attender';
  const type = document.getElementById('att_type').value;
  const msg = document.getElementById('att_msg').value.trim();
  if (!msg) {
    showToast("Please describe your concern before submitting");
    return;
  }

  const newConcern = {
    id: Date.now(),
    pid,
    attender: name,
    t: Date.now(),
    type,
    text: msg,
    status: 'unreviewed',
    reviewedBy: null,
    reviewNote: null
  };

  S.concerns.unshift(newConcern);
  logAction(`New attender concern submitted by ${name}`, "Attender");
  saveState();
  nav('a_concerns');
  showToast(t('btn_submit_concern'));
}

function markMedVerified(medId) {
  const m = S.medReviews.find(x => x.id === medId);
  if (!m) return;
  m.status = 'verified';
  logAction(`Medication ${m.med} verified & administered by ${U.name}`, "Clinical");
  saveState();
  draw();
  showToast(`Verified: ${m.med}`);
}

function saveQuickVitals() {
  const pid = +document.getElementById('qv_pid').value;
  const hr = document.getElementById('qv_hr').value;
  const spo2 = document.getElementById('qv_spo2').value;
  const temp = document.getElementById('qv_temp').value;

  if (hr) pushVital(pid, 'hr', hr, 'manual');
  if (spo2) pushVital(pid, 'spo2', spo2, 'manual');
  if (temp) pushVital(pid, 'temp', temp, 'manual');

  evaluatePatient(getPatient(pid));
  logAction(`Quick bedside vitals entered for patient #${pid} by ${U.name}`, "Clinical");
  nav('n_triage');
  showToast(t('btn_save'));
}

function reportPatientSymptom(pid) {
  const p = getPatient(pid);
  p.status = 'unchecked';
  S.al.unshift({
    id: Date.now(),
    pid,
    score: 4,
    t: Date.now(),
    level: 'high',
    cat: 'Bedside Call Alert',
    desc: `Bedside call assistance requested by ${p.name} from patient portal`,
    res: null,
    assigned: p.nurse || 'Nurse'
  });
  logAction(`Bedside call assistance triggered by patient ${p.name}`, "Alert");
  saveState();
  draw();
  showToast(t('btn_call_nurse') + ": " + t('status_unchecked'));
}

function exportAuditLog() {
  const rows = [
    ['Timestamp', 'User', 'Role', 'Category', 'Action'],
    ...S.au.map(a => [tm(a.t), a.u, a.role || 'STAFF', a.cat || 'Clinical', `"${a.a.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`])
  ];
  const csvContent = "data:text/csv;charset=utf-8," + rows.map(e => e.join(",")).join("\n");
  const encodedUri = encodeURI(csvContent);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.setAttribute("href", encodedUri);
  link.setAttribute("download", `CLYNORA_Audit_Trail_${Date.now()}.csv`);
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  showToast("Audit trail exported to CSV");
}

// Mouse Follower Init (with safe checks)
const cd = (typeof document !== 'undefined') ? document.getElementById('cd') : null;
const cr = (typeof document !== 'undefined') ? document.getElementById('cr') : null;
let mx = 0, my = 0, rx = 0, ry = 0;

if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    mx = e.clientX;
    my = e.clientY;
    if (cd && cd.style) {
      cd.style.left = `${mx}px`;
      cd.style.top = `${my}px`;
    }
    if (cr && cr.classList) {
      cr.classList.toggle('h', !!e.target.closest('button, select, input, .role-card, a, [onclick], .prompt-chip, .voice-prompt-chip'));
    }
  });

  (function animateCursor() {
    rx += (mx - rx) * 0.16;
    ry += (my - ry) * 0.16;
    if (cr && cr.style) {
      cr.style.left = `${rx}px`;
      cr.style.top = `${ry}px`;
    }
    if (typeof requestAnimationFrame === 'function') {
      requestAnimationFrame(animateCursor);
    }
  })();
}

// Initialize Navigation and Session on Load
updateNavHeader();
if (U) {
  openApp();
}
















--- FILE: js\assistant.js ---
// CLYNORA AI Assistant (Text & Voice Launcher)
// Role-aware bedside clinical decision support engine
// Multilingual support: English (en), Tamil (ta), Hindi (hi)

let assistantOpen = false;
let assistantMessages = [];

function initAssistant() {
  const container = document.getElementById('assistantDrawer');
  if (!container) return;

  // Initial welcome message if empty
  if (assistantMessages.length === 0) {
    assistantMessages.push({
      sender: 'bot',
      text: getWelcomeMessage(),
      time: Date.now()
    });
  }

  renderAssistantMessages();
  renderRolePrompts();
}

function getWelcomeMessage() {
  const role = window.U ? window.U.role : 'doc';

  if (role === 'doc') {
    return "Welcome. I can summarize patient deterioration, review concerns, or analyze telemetry. Select a prompt or type below.";
  }
  if (role === 'nurse') {
    return "Welcome. I can help review triage alerts, patient concerns, and medication verification requests.";
  }
  if (role === 'pat') {
    return "Welcome to your CLYNORA health assistant. I can explain your recorded vital signs or guide you on requesting assistance.";
  }
  if (role === 'att') {
    return "Welcome. I can help you review the patient’s available monitoring updates and submitted concerns.";
  }
  return "CLYNORA Assistant ready. How can I assist?";
}

function toggleAssistantDrawer(forceOpen) {
  assistantOpen = forceOpen !== undefined ? forceOpen : !assistantOpen;
  const drawer = document.getElementById('assistantDrawer');
  const fab = document.getElementById('assistantFab');
  if (!drawer || !fab) return;

  if (assistantOpen) {
    drawer.classList.add('open');
    fab.classList.add('active');
    initAssistant();
    const input = document.getElementById('assistantInput');
    if (input) setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 200);
  } else {
    drawer.classList.remove('open');
    fab.classList.remove('active');
  }
}

function renderRolePrompts() {
  const container = document.getElementById('assistantPrompts');
  if (!container) return;

  const role = window.U ? window.U.role : 'doc';
  let prompts = [];

  if (role === 'doc') {
    prompts = [
      t('prompt_doc_1'),
      t('prompt_doc_2'),
      t('prompt_doc_3')
    ];
  } else if (role === 'nurse') {
    prompts = [
      t('prompt_nurse_1'),
      t('prompt_nurse_2'),
      t('prompt_nurse_3')
    ];
  } else if (role === 'pat') {
    prompts = [
      t('prompt_pat_1'),
      t('prompt_pat_2')
    ];
  } else if (role === 'att') {
    prompts = [
      t('prompt_att_1'),
      t('prompt_att_2')
    ];
  } else {
    prompts = [
      t('prompt_doc_1'),
      t('prompt_nurse_1')
    ];
  }

  container.innerHTML = prompts.map(p => `
    <button class="prompt-chip" onclick="sendAssistantPrompt('${p.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
      ${p}
    </button>
  `).join('');
}

function renderAssistantMessages() {
  const container = document.getElementById('assistantMessages');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = assistantMessages.map(m => `
    <div class="assistant-msg ${m.sender === 'user' ? 'user-msg' : 'bot-msg'}">
      <div class="msg-content">${m.text}</div>
      <div class="msg-time">${tm(m.time, 'timeOnly')}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
}

function sendAssistantPrompt(text) {
  const input = document.getElementById('assistantInput');
  if (input) input.value = text;
  handleAssistantSubmit();
}

function handleAssistantSubmit() {
  const input = document.getElementById('assistantInput');
  if (!input) return;
  const q = input.value.trim();
  if (!q) return;

  input.value = '';

  // Append user message
  assistantMessages.push({
    sender: 'user',
    text: q,
    time: Date.now()
  });
  renderAssistantMessages();

  // Show typing indicator
  const container = document.getElementById('assistantMessages');
  const typingEl = document.createElement('div');
  typingEl.className = 'assistant-msg bot-msg typing';
  typingEl.id = 'botTyping';
  typingEl.innerHTML = '<div class="typing-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>';
  container.appendChild(typingEl);
  container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;

  // Process response
  setTimeout(() => {
    const typing = document.getElementById('botTyping');
    if (typing) typing.remove();

    const answer = generateAIAnswer(q);
    assistantMessages.push({
      sender: 'bot',
      text: answer,
      time: Date.now()
    });
    renderAssistantMessages();
  }, 650);
}

// Context-aware Clinical Rules Engine with multilingual support
function generateAIAnswer(query) {
  const q = (query || '').toLowerCase();
  const activePid = (window.U && window.U.pid) ? window.U.pid : (window.cur || 101);
  const patient = (typeof getPatient === 'function') ? (getPatient(activePid) || (window.S && window.S.p && window.S.p[0])) : null;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'en';

  // Helper check for multilingual query keywords
  const isVitalsQuery = q.includes('summar') || q.includes('recent reading') || q.includes('vitals') || 
                       q.includes('heart rate') || q.includes('oxygen') || q.includes('spo2') ||
                       q.includes('à®šà¯à®°à¯à®•à¯à®•à®¿') || q.includes('à®…à®³à®µà¯€à®Ÿà¯') || q.includes('à®‡à®¤à®¯à®¤à¯à®¤à¯à®Ÿà®¿à®ªà¯à®ªà¯') ||

                          q.includes('à®•à®µà®²à¯ˆ') || q.includes('à®•à¯‹à®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆ') || q.includes('à®ªà¯à®•à®¾à®°à¯') ||
                          q.includes('à¤šà¤¿à¤‚à¤¤à¤¾') || q.includes('à¤¶à¤¿à¤•à¤¾à¤¯à¤¤') || q.includes('à¤…à¤¨à¤¸à¥à¤²à¤à¥€');

                               q.includes('à®†à®ªà®¤à¯à®¤à¯') || q.includes('à®Žà®šà¯à®šà®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆ à®¨à®¿à®²à¯ˆ') || q.includes('à®‰à®Ÿà®©à®Ÿà®¿ à®•à®µà®©à®®à¯') ||
                               q.includes('à¤—à¤¿à¤°à¤¾à¤µà¤Ÿ') || q.includes('à¤šà¥‡à¤¤à¤¾à¤µà¤¨à¥€ à¤¸à¥€à¤®à¤¾') || q.includes('à¤¤à¥à¤°à¤‚à¤¤ à¤§à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤¨');

  const isMedsQuery = q.includes('medication') || q.includes('prescription') || q.includes('drug') ||
                      q.includes('à®®à®°à¯à®¨à¯à®¤à¯') || q.includes('à®šà®°à®¿à®ªà®¾à®°à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà¯') ||

                      q.includes('à®¤à¯Šà®Ÿà®°à¯à®ªà¯') || q.includes('à®šà¯†à®µà®¿à®²à®¿à®¯à®°à¯') || q.includes('à®…à®´à¯ˆà®•à¯à®•') ||
                      q.includes('à¤¨à¤°à¥à¤¸') || q.includes('à¤®à¤¦à¤¦') || q.includes('à¤¬à¥à¤²à¤¾à¤à¤‚') || q.includes('à¤¸à¤‚à¤ªà¤°à¥à¤•');

  if (isVitalsQuery) {
    if (!patient) return lang === 'ta' ? "à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿ à®¤à¯‡à®°à¯à®¨à¯à®¤à¯†à®Ÿà¯à®•à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà®µà®¿à®²à¯à®²à¯ˆ." : lang === 'hi' ? "à¤•à¥‹à¤ˆ à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ à¤šà¤¯à¤¨à¤¿à¤¤ à¤¨à¤¹à¥€à¤‚ à¤¹à¥ˆà¥¤" : "No active patient selected for vital sign analysis.";

    const temp = (typeof getLatestVital === 'function') ? getLatestVital(patient.id, 'temp') : null;
    const ews = (typeof calcEWS === 'function') ? calcEWS(patient) : { total: 0, alert: false };

    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      return `<strong>${patient.name} (à¤µà¤¾à¤°à¥à¤¡ ${patient.ward}/à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡ ${patient.bed}) à¤•à¤¾ à¤•à¥à¤²à¤¿à¤¨à¤¿à¤•à¤² à¤¸à¤¾à¤°à¤¾à¤‚à¤¶:</strong><br>` +
        `\u2022 à¤¹à¥ƒà¤¦à¤¯ à¤—à¤¤à¤¿: ${hr ? hr.v + ' bpm' : 'N/A'} (à¤¬à¥‡à¤¸à¤²à¤¾à¤‡à¤¨: ${patient.base || 'à¤¸à¥‡à¤Ÿ à¤¨à¤¹à¥€à¤‚'} bpm)<br>` +
        `\u2022 SpO\u2082‚ à¤‘à¤•à¥à¤¸à¥€à¤œà¤¨: ${spo2 ? spo2.v + ' %' : 'N/A'}<br>` +
        `\u2022 à¤¶à¤°à¥€à¤° à¤•à¤¾ à¤¤à¤¾à¤ªà¤®à¤¾à¤¨: ${temp ? temp.v + ' \u00B0C' : 'N/A'}<br>` +
        `\u2022 à¤ªà¥à¤°à¤¾à¤°à¤‚à¤­à¤¿à¤• à¤šà¥‡à¤¤à¤¾à¤µà¤¨à¥€ à¤¸à¥à¤•à¥‹à¤°: <strong>${ews.total}</strong> (${ews.alert ? 'âš ï¸ à¤—à¤¿à¤°à¤¾à¤µà¤Ÿ à¤¸à¥€à¤®à¤¾ à¤ªà¤¾à¤° à¤¹à¥à¤ˆ - à¤¤à¥à¤°à¤‚à¤¤ à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡à¤¸à¤¾à¤‡à¤¡ à¤¸à¤®à¥€à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¾ à¤•à¤°à¥‡à¤‚' : '\u{2705} à¤¸à¥à¤°à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¿à¤¤ à¤¸à¥€à¤®à¤¾à¤“à¤‚ à¤•à¥‡ à¤­à¥€à¤¤à¤°'})<br>` +
        `<small class="mut">à¤¯à¤¹ à¤†à¤‰à¤Ÿà¤ªà¥à¤Ÿ à¤Ÿà¥‡à¤²à¥€à¤®à¥‡à¤Ÿà¥à¤°à¥€ à¤¨à¤®à¥‚à¤¨à¤¾ à¤¡à¥‡à¤Ÿà¤¾ à¤ªà¤° à¤†à¤§à¤¾à¤°à¤¿à¤¤ à¤¹à¥ˆà¥¤ à¤•à¥‡à¤µà¤² à¤¨à¤¿à¤°à¥à¤£à¤¯ à¤¸à¤®à¤°à¥à¤¥à¤¨ à¤•à¥‡ à¤²à¤¿à¤à¥¤</small>`;
    }

    return `<strong>Clinical Summary for ${patient.name} (Ward ${patient.ward}/Bed ${patient.bed}):</strong><br>` +
      `\u2022 Heart Rate: ${hr ? hr.v + ' bpm' : 'N/A'} (Baseline: ${patient.base || 'Not set'} bpm)<br>` +
      `\u2022 SpO\u2082: ${spo2 ? spo2.v + ' %' : 'N/A'}<br>` +
      `\u2022 Core Temperature: ${temp ? temp.v + ' \u00B0C' : 'N/A'}<br>` +
      `\u2022 Early Warning Score: <strong>${ews.total}</strong> (${ews.alert ? '\u{26A0}\u{FE0F} Deterioration Threshold Crossed' : '\u{2705} Within safe parameters'})<br>` +
      `<small class="mut">Decision support output based on wearable telemetry. Last updated: ${hr ? tm(hr.t) : 'Never'}.</small>`;
  }

  // 2. Unreviewed Clinical Concerns
  if (isConcernsQuery) {
    const unrev = (window.S && window.S.concerns) ? window.S.concerns.filter(c => c.status === 'unreviewed') : [];
    if (unrev.length === 0) {
      if (lang === 'ta') return "\u{2705} à®¤à®±à¯à®ªà¯‹à®¤à¯ à®¨à®¿à®²à¯à®µà¯ˆà®¯à®¿à®²à¯ à®‰à®³à¯à®³ à®•à®µà®²à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯ à®à®¤à¯à®®à®¿à®²à¯à®²à¯ˆ. à®…à®©à¯ˆà®¤à¯à®¤à¯ à®•à¯‹à®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯à®®à¯ à®šà¯†à®µà®¿à®²à®¿à®¯à®°à®¾à®²à¯ à®ªà®°à®¿à®šà¯€à®²à®¿à®•à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà¯à®³à¯à®³à®©.";
      if (lang === 'hi') return "\u{2705} à¤µà¤°à¥à¤¤à¤®à¤¾à¤¨ à¤®à¥‡à¤‚ à¤•à¥‹à¤ˆ à¤…à¤¨à¤¸à¥à¤²à¤à¥€ à¤šà¤¿à¤‚à¤¤à¤¾à¤à¤‚ à¤¨à¤¹à¥€à¤‚ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤ à¤¸à¤­à¥€ à¤¤à¥€à¤®à¤¾à¤°à¤¦à¤¾à¤° à¤…à¤¨à¥à¤°à¥‹à¤§à¥‹à¤‚ à¤ªà¤° à¤§à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤¨ à¤¦à¤¿à¤¯à¤¾ à¤—à¤¯à¤¾ à¤¹à¥ˆà¥¤";
      return "\u{2705} There are currently no unreviewed concerns. All submitted attender requests have been attended by nursing staff.";
    }

    if (lang === 'ta') {
      const list = unrev.map(c => {
        const p = getPatient(c.pid);
        return `\u2022 <strong>${p ? p.name : 'à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿'} (à®µà®¾à®°à¯à®Ÿà¯ ${p ? p.ward + '/' + p.bed : '\u2014'}):</strong> ${c.type} \u2014 "${c.text}" (à®…à®©à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà®¿à®¯à®µà®°à¯: ${c.attender})`;
      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à®ªà®°à®¿à®šà¯€à®²à®¿à®•à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà®¾à®¤ à®•à¯‹à®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯ (${unrev.length}):</strong><br>${list}<br><small class="mut">à®µà®¾à®°à¯à®Ÿà¯ à®•à®µà®²à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯ à®ªà®•à¯à®•à®¤à¯à®¤à®¿à®²à¯ à®‡à®¤à®©à¯ˆ à®šà®°à®¿à®ªà®¾à®°à¯à®•à¯à®•à®µà¯à®®à¯.</small>`;
    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      const list = unrev.map(c => {
        const p = getPatient(c.pid);
        return `\u2022 <strong>${p ? p.name : 'à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ'} (à¤µà¤¾à¤°à¥à¤¡ ${p ? p.ward + '/' + p.bed : '\u2014'}):</strong> ${c.type} \u2014 "${c.text}" (à¤ªà¥à¤°à¤¸à¥à¤¤à¥à¤¤à¤•à¤°à¥à¤¤à¤¾: ${c.attender})`;
      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à¤…à¤¨à¤¸à¥à¤²à¤à¥€ à¤¤à¥€à¤®à¤¾à¤°à¤¦à¤¾à¤° à¤šà¤¿à¤‚à¤¤à¤¾à¤à¤‚ (${unrev.length}):</strong><br>${list}<br><small class="mut">à¤•à¥ƒà¤ªà¤¯à¤¾ à¤µà¤¾à¤°à¥à¤¡ à¤šà¤¿à¤‚à¤¤à¤¾à¤à¤‚ à¤Ÿà¥ˆà¤¬ à¤®à¥‡à¤‚ à¤¸à¤®à¥€à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¾ à¤•à¤°à¥‡à¤‚à¥¤</small>`;
    }

    const list = unrev.map(c => {
      const p = getPatient(c.pid);
      return `\u2022 <strong>${p ? p.name : 'Unknown'} (${p ? p.ward + '/' + p.bed : 'N/A'}):</strong> ${c.type} \u2014 "${c.text}" (Submitted by ${c.attender}, ${timeAgo(c.t)})`;

  }

  // 3. Deterioration & Immediate Attention
  if (isDeteriorationQuery) {
    const attentionList = (window.S && window.S.p) ? window.S.p.filter(p => p.on && p.status === 'unchecked') : [];
    if (attentionList.length === 0) {
      if (lang === 'ta') return "à®…à®©à¯ˆà®¤à¯à®¤à¯ à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿à®•à®³à¯à®®à¯ à®¤à®±à¯à®ªà¯‹à®¤à¯ à®¨à®¿à®²à¯ˆà®¯à®¾à®© à®¨à®¿à®²à¯ˆà®¯à®¿à®²à¯ à®‰à®³à¯à®³à®©à®°à¯.";
      if (lang === 'hi') return "à¤¸à¤­à¥€ à¤¨à¤¿à¤—à¤°à¤¾à¤¨à¥€ à¤®à¥‡à¤‚ à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ à¤µà¤°à¥à¤¤à¤®à¤¾à¤¨ à¤®à¥‡à¤‚ à¤¸à¥à¤¥à¤¿à¤° à¤¸à¥à¤¥à¤¿à¤¤à¤¿ à¤®à¥‡à¤‚ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤";
      return "All monitored patients are currently stable with checked status.";
    }

    if (lang === 'ta') {
      const details = attentionList.map(p => {

        const hr = getLatestVital(p.id, 'hr');

      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à®‰à®Ÿà®©à®Ÿà®¿ à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆ à®µà®´à®¿ à®•à®µà®©à®®à¯ à®¤à¯‡à®µà¯ˆà®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®®à¯ à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿à®•à®³à¯ (${attentionList.length}):</strong><br>${details}<br><small class="mut">à®ªà®°à®¿à®¨à¯à®¤à¯à®°à¯ˆ: à®‰à®Ÿà®©à®Ÿà®¿ à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆ à®µà®´à®¿ à®šà¯‹à®¤à®©à¯ˆ à®®à®±à¯à®±à¯à®®à¯ à®…à®³à®µà¯€à®Ÿà¯ à®šà®°à®¿à®ªà®¾à®°à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà¯.</small>`;
    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      const details = attentionList.map(p => {
        const ews = calcEWS(p);
        const hr = getLatestVital(p.id, 'hr');
        return `\u2022 <strong>${p.name}</strong> \u00B7 à¤µà¤¾à¤°à¥à¤¡ ${p.ward} à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡ ${p.bed} (EWS à¤¸à¥à¤•à¥‹à¤°: ${ews.total}) \u2014 ${hr ? hr.v + ' bpm' : ''}`;

      return `<strong>à¤¤à¥à¤°à¤‚à¤¤ à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡à¤¸à¤¾à¤‡à¤¡ à¤§à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤¨ à¤¦à¥‡à¤¨à¥‡ à¤¯à¥‹à¤—à¥à¤¯ à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ (${attentionList.length}):</strong><br>${details}<br><small class="mut">à¤…à¤¨à¥à¤¶à¤‚à¤¸à¤¿à¤¤ à¤•à¤¾à¤°à¥à¤°à¤µà¤¾à¤ˆ: à¤¤à¥à¤°à¤‚à¤¤ à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡à¤¸à¤¾à¤‡à¤¡ à¤Ÿà¥à¤°à¤¾à¤‡à¤à¤œ à¤”à¤° à¤µà¤¾à¤‡à¤Ÿà¤² à¤¸à¤¤à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤ªà¤¨à¥¤</small>`;
    }

    const details = attentionList.map(p => {
      const ews = calcEWS(p);
      const hr = getLatestVital(p.id, 'hr');
      return `\u2022 <strong>${p.name}</strong> \u00B7 Ward ${p.ward} Bed ${p.bed} (EWS Score: ${ews.total}) \u2014 ${hr ? hr.v + ' bpm' : ''}`;
    }).join('<br>');
    return `<strong>Patients Requiring Immediate Bedside Attention (${attentionList.length}):</strong><br>${details}<br><small class="mut">Recommended action: Immediate bedside triage check and vital sign verification.</small>`;
  }

  // 4. Alerts Queue
  if (isAlertsQuery) {
    const openAlerts = (window.S && window.S.al) ? window.S.al.filter(a => !a.res) : [];
    if (openAlerts.length === 0) {
      if (lang === 'ta') return "à®…à®©à¯ˆà®¤à¯à®¤à¯ à®®à®°à¯à®¤à¯à®¤à¯à®µ à®Žà®šà¯à®šà®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯à®®à¯ à®ªà®°à®¿à®šà¯€à®²à®¿à®•à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà¯ à®à®±à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà¯à®³à¯à®³à®©.";
      if (lang === 'hi') return "à¤¸à¤­à¥€ à¤•à¥à¤²à¤¿à¤¨à¤¿à¤•à¤² à¤…à¤²à¤°à¥à¤Ÿ à¤•à¥€ à¤¸à¤®à¥€à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¾ à¤•à¥€ à¤œà¤¾ à¤šà¥à¤•à¥€ à¤¹à¥ˆ à¤”à¤° à¤¸à¥à¤µà¥€à¤•à¤¾à¤° à¤•à¤° à¤²à¤¿à¤ à¤—à¤ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤";

    }

    if (lang === 'ta') {
      const list = openAlerts.map(a => {

        return `\u2022 <strong>[${(a.level || 'alert').toUpperCase()}] ${p ? p.name : 'à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿'}:</strong> ${a.cat} \u2014 ${a.desc}`;
      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à®šà¯†à®¯à®²à®¿à®²à¯ à®‰à®³à¯à®³ à®à®±à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà®¾à®¤ à®Žà®šà¯à®šà®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯ (${openAlerts.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      const list = openAlerts.map(a => {
        const p = getPatient(a.pid);
        return `\u2022 <strong>[${(a.level || 'alert').toUpperCase()}] ${p ? p.name : 'à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ'}:</strong> ${a.cat} \u2014 ${a.desc}`;
      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à¤¸à¤•à¥à¤°à¤¿à¤¯ à¤…à¤¨à¤¸à¥à¤µà¥€à¤•à¥ƒà¤¤ à¤…à¤²à¤°à¥à¤Ÿ (${openAlerts.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
    }

    const list = openAlerts.map(a => {
      const p = getPatient(a.pid);
      return `\u2022 <strong>[${a.level.toUpperCase()}] ${p ? p.name : 'Patient'}:</strong> ${a.cat} \u2014 ${a.desc} (${timeAgo(a.t)})`;
    }).join('<br>');
    return `<strong>Active Unacknowledged Alerts (${openAlerts.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
  }

  // 5. Medication Review
  if (isMedsQuery) {
    const pendingMeds = (window.S && window.S.medReviews) ? window.S.medReviews.filter(m => m.status === 'pending') : [];
    if (pendingMeds.length === 0) {
      if (lang === 'ta') return "à®…à®©à¯ˆà®¤à¯à®¤à¯ à®·à®¿à®ªà¯à®Ÿà¯ à®®à®°à¯à®¨à¯à®¤à¯à®•à®³à¯à®®à¯ à®šà®°à®¿à®ªà®¾à®°à¯à®•à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà¯ à®µà®´à®™à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà¯à®³à¯à®³à®©.";
      if (lang === 'hi') return "à¤‡à¤¸ à¤¶à¤¿à¤«à¥à¤Ÿ à¤•à¥€ à¤¸à¤­à¥€ à¤¦à¤µà¤¾à¤à¤‚ à¤¸à¤¤à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤ªà¤¿à¤¤ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚ à¤”à¤° à¤¸à¤®à¤¯ à¤ªà¤° à¤¦à¥€ à¤œà¤¾ à¤šà¥à¤•à¥€ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤";
      return "All shift medications are verified and up to date.";
    }

    if (lang === 'ta') {
      const list = pendingMeds.map(m => {
        const p = getPatient(m.pid);
        return `\u2022 <strong>${m.med}</strong> - ${p ? p.name : 'à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿'} (à®¨à¯‡à®°à®®à¯: ${m.due}) \u2014 ${m.notes}`;
      }).join('<br>');
      return `<strong>à®¨à®¿à®²à¯à®µà¯ˆà®¯à®¿à®²à¯ à®‰à®³à¯à®³ à®®à®°à¯à®¨à¯à®¤à¯ à®šà®°à®¿à®ªà®¾à®°à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà¯à®•à®³à¯ (${pendingMeds.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      const list = pendingMeds.map(m => {
        const p = getPatient(m.pid);
        return `\u2022 <strong>${m.med}</strong> - ${p ? p.name : 'à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ'} (à¤¸à¤®à¤¯: ${m.due}) \u2014 ${m.notes}`;

      return `<strong>à¤²à¤‚à¤¬à¤¿à¤¤ à¤¦à¤µà¤¾ à¤¸à¤®à¥€à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¾à¤à¤‚ (${pendingMeds.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
    }

    const list = pendingMeds.map(m => {
      const p = getPatient(m.pid);
      return `\u2022 <strong>${m.med}</strong> for ${p ? p.name : 'Patient'} (Due: ${m.due}) \u2014 ${m.notes}`;
    }).join('<br>');
    return `<strong>Pending Medication Reviews (${pendingMeds.length}):</strong><br>${list}`;
  }

  // 6. Patient / Attender Help
  if (isHelpQuery) {
    if (lang === 'ta') {
      return "à®‰à®™à¯à®•à®³à¯ à®šà¯†à®µà®¿à®²à®¿à®¯à®°à¯ <strong>à®šà¯†à®µà®¿à®²à®¿à®¯à®°à¯ à®ªà®¿à®°à®¿à®¯à®¾ à®¨à®¾à®¯à®°à¯</strong> (à®¨à®°à¯à®šà®¿à®™à¯ à®¨à®¿à®²à¯ˆà®¯à®®à¯ 3). à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆ à®…à®´à¯ˆà®ªà¯à®ªà¯ à®ªà¯Šà®¤à¯à®¤à®¾à®©à¯ˆ à®…à®´à¯à®¤à¯à®¤à®²à®¾à®®à¯ à®…à®²à¯à®²à®¤à¯ à®‰à®¤à®µà®¿à®¯à®¾à®³à®°à¯ à®¤à®³à®®à¯ à®µà®´à®¿à®¯à®¾à®• à®•à¯‹à®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®¯à¯ˆ à®…à®©à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà®²à®¾à®®à¯.";
    } else if (lang === 'hi') {
      return "à¤†à¤ªà¤•à¥€ à¤‰à¤ªà¤¸à¥à¤¥à¤¿à¤¤ à¤¨à¤°à¥à¤¸ <strong>à¤¨à¤°à¥à¤¸ à¤ªà¥à¤°à¤¿à¤¯à¤¾ à¤¨à¤¾à¤¯à¤°</strong> (à¤¨à¤°à¥à¤¸à¤¿à¤‚à¤— à¤¸à¥à¤Ÿà¥‡à¤¶à¤¨ 3) à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤ à¤†à¤ª à¤¬à¥‡à¤¡à¤¸à¤¾à¤‡à¤¡ à¤•à¥‰à¤² à¤¬à¤Ÿà¤¨ à¤¦à¤¬à¤¾ à¤¸à¤•à¤¤à¥‡ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚ à¤¯à¤¾ à¤¸à¥€à¤§à¥‡ à¤…à¤¨à¥à¤°à¥‹à¤§ à¤¦à¤°à¥à¤œ à¤•à¤° à¤¸à¤•à¤¤à¥‡ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤";
    }
    return "You can press the physical bedside call button or submit a concern in your Attender Portal to notify the nursing station.";
  }

  // Default response
  if (lang === 'ta') {
    return "à®¨à®¾à®©à¯ à®¤à®±à¯à®ªà¯‹à®¤à¯ˆà®¯ à®µà®¾à®°à¯à®Ÿà¯ à®¤à®°à®µà¯ˆ à®†à®¯à¯à®µà¯ à®šà¯†à®¯à¯à®¤à¯à®³à¯à®³à¯‡à®©à¯. à®…à®³à®µà¯€à®Ÿà¯à®•à®³à¯ˆ à®šà¯à®°à¯à®•à¯à®•à®¿ à®•à¯‚à®±à®µà¯à®®à¯, à®†à®ªà®¤à¯à®¤à¯ à®¨à¯‹à®¯à®¾à®³à®¿à®•à®³à¯ˆà®ªà¯ à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®Ÿà®¿à®¯à®²à®¿à®Ÿà®µà¯à®®à¯ à®…à®²à¯à®²à®¤à¯ à®Žà®šà¯à®šà®°à®¿à®•à¯à®•à¯ˆà®•à®³à¯ˆ à®†à®¯à¯à®µà¯ à®šà¯†à®¯à¯à®¯à®µà¯à®®à¯ à®Žà®©à¯à®©à®¿à®Ÿà®®à¯ à®•à¯‡à®Ÿà¯à®•à®²à®¾à®®à¯.";
  } else if (lang === 'hi') {
    return "à¤®à¥ˆà¤‚à¤¨à¥‡ à¤µà¤¾à¤°à¥à¤¡ à¤°à¤¿à¤•à¥‰à¤°à¥à¤¡à¥à¤¸ à¤•à¥€ à¤œà¤¾à¤‚à¤š à¤•à¤° à¤²à¥€ à¤¹à¥ˆà¥¤ à¤†à¤ª à¤®à¥à¤à¤¸à¥‡ à¤µà¤¾à¤‡à¤Ÿà¤² à¤•à¤¾ à¤¸à¤¾à¤°à¤¾à¤‚à¤¶, à¤§à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤¨ à¤¦à¥‡à¤¨à¥‡ à¤¯à¥‹à¤—à¥à¤¯ à¤®à¤°à¥€à¤œ à¤¯à¤¾ à¤…à¤²à¤°à¥à¤Ÿà¥à¤¸ à¤•à¥€ à¤¸à¤®à¥€à¤•à¥à¤·à¤¾ à¤•à¥‡ à¤²à¤¿à¤ à¤ªà¥‚à¤› à¤¸à¤•à¤¤à¥‡ à¤¹à¥ˆà¤‚à¥¤";
  }
  return "I have checked the current ward telemetry records. You can ask me to summarize vitals, list unreviewed concerns, or check which patients require attention.";
}

// CLYNORA ECG Canvas Monitor Engine
// Renders calibrated clinical Lead II ECG with authentic P-Q-R-S-T wave physiology,
// sweep erase bar, medical grid paper, and direct AD8232 serial data support.

class CLYNORA_ECGMonitor {
  constructor(canvasId, options = {}) {
    this.canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
    this.options = Object.assign({
      grid: true,
      sweepSpeed: 1.5,
      gain: 1.0,
      color: '#f2545b', // CLYNORA pulse coral
      glow: true
    }, options);

    this.points = [];
    this.sweepX = 0;
    this.running = true;
    this.phase = 0;
    this.animationId = null;

    this.resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());
    this.animate();
  }

  resize() {
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    const rect = this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    this.width = rect.width || 600;
    this.height = 160;
    this.canvas.width = this.width * dpr;
    this.canvas.height = this.height * dpr;
    this.canvas.style.width = `${this.width}px`;
    this.canvas.style.height = `${this.height}px`;
    this.ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);

    // Initialize point buffer
    this.buffer = new Float32Array(Math.floor(this.width));
    this.buffer.fill(this.height / 2);
  }

  // Authentic Lead II P-Q-R-S-T physiological wave function
  getWaveSample(t, hr = 72) {
    const cycleTime = 60 / hr; // Seconds per beat
    const phase = (t % cycleTime) / cycleTime; // 0 to 1

    let amp = 0;

    // P wave (Atrial Depolarization): 0.10 - 0.20
    if (phase > 0.10 && phase < 0.22) {
      const pProgress = (phase - 0.10) / 0.12;
      amp = Math.sin(pProgress * Math.PI) * 0.18;
    }
    // PR Segment: baseline 0.22 - 0.32
    else if (phase >= 0.22 && phase < 0.32) {
      amp = 0;
    }
    // Q wave (Septal Depolarization): 0.32 - 0.35
    else if (phase >= 0.32 && phase < 0.35) {
      const qProgress = (phase - 0.32) / 0.03;
      amp = -Math.sin(qProgress * Math.PI) * 0.15;
    }
    // R wave (Ventricular Depolarization Spike): 0.35 - 0.41
    else if (phase >= 0.35 && phase < 0.41) {
      const rProgress = (phase - 0.35) / 0.06;
      amp = Math.sin(rProgress * Math.PI) * 1.0;
    }
    // S wave (Late Ventricular Depolarization): 0.41 - 0.46
    else if (phase >= 0.41 && phase < 0.46) {
      const sProgress = (phase - 0.41) / 0.05;
      amp = -Math.sin(sProgress * Math.PI) * 0.28;
    }
    // ST Segment: 0.46 - 0.54
    else if (phase >= 0.46 && phase < 0.54) {
      amp = 0.02;
    }
    // T wave (Ventricular Repolarization): 0.54 - 0.72
    else if (phase >= 0.54 && phase < 0.72) {
      const tProgress = (phase - 0.54) / 0.18;
      amp = Math.sin(tProgress * Math.PI) * 0.32;
    }
    // Isoelectric baseline until next beat
    else {
      amp = 0;
    }

    // Add minute physiological baseline wander
    const wander = Math.sin(t * 0.8) * 0.03;
    return amp + wander;
  }

  animate() {
    if (!this.running) return;

    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const w = this.width;
    const h = this.height;
    const midY = h / 2;

    // Get current patient's heart rate
    let hr = 72;
    if (typeof cur !== 'undefined' && typeof getPatient === 'function') {
      const p = getPatient(cur);
      if (p) {
        const vit = (typeof getLatestVital === 'function') ? getLatestVital(p.id, 'hr') : null;
        hr = vit ? vit.v : (p.base || 72);
      }
    }

    // Advance sweep
    const step = 2;
    for (let s = 0; s < step; s++) {
      this.phase += 0.005;
      let sampleY = 0;

      // Check if real AD8232 serial data is in L.ecg
      if (typeof L !== 'undefined' && L.ecg && L.ecg.length > 0) {
        const raw = L.ecg.shift();
        // AD8232 10-bit or 12-bit ADC normalizer
        const normalized = (raw - 512) / 300;
        sampleY = midY - (normalized * (h * 0.35) * this.options.gain);
      } else {
        // High fidelity physiological simulation
        const val = this.getWaveSample(this.phase, hr);
        sampleY = midY - (val * (h * 0.42) * this.options.gain);
      }

      const idx = Math.floor(this.sweepX);
      if (idx < this.buffer.length) {
        this.buffer[idx] = sampleY;
      }

      this.sweepX = (this.sweepX + 1) % w;
    }

    // Render Canvas Frame
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Draw ECG Medical Grid
    if (this.options.grid) {
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(58, 91, 217, 0.07)';
      // 10px minor grid
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 10) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(x, h);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 10) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, y);
        ctx.lineTo(w, y);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      // 50px major grid
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(58, 91, 217, 0.15)';
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 50) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(x, h);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 50) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, y);
        ctx.lineTo(w, y);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }

    // Draw ECG Waveform with glow
    ctx.save();
    if (this.options.glow) {
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(242, 84, 91, 0.55)';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 6;
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.options.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

    ctx.beginPath();
    let gap = 24; // Erase bar width ahead of sweep
    let started = false;

    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      // Don't draw in the sweep gap
      const distFromSweep = (x - this.sweepX + w) % w;
      if (distFromSweep < gap) {
        started = false;
        continue;
      }

      const y = this.buffer[x] || midY;
      if (!started) {
        ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        started = true;
      } else {
        ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      }
    }
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();

    // Draw Sweep Erase Bar Line & Glow
    const sweepGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(this.sweepX - gap, 0, this.sweepX + 4, 0);
    sweepGradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(238, 240, 246, 0)');
    sweepGradient.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(238, 240, 246, 0.95)');
    sweepGradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(242, 84, 91, 0.8)');

    ctx.fillStyle = sweepGradient;
    ctx.fillRect(Math.max(0, this.sweepX - gap), 0, gap + 4, h);

    this.animationId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.animate());
  }

  destroy() {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.animationId) cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationId);
  }
}

// Global monitor reference
let globalECGMonitor = null;

function initECG(canvasId = 'ecgCanvas') {
  if (globalECGMonitor) {
    globalECGMonitor.destroy();
  }
  const el = document.getElementById(canvasId);
  if (el) {
    globalECGMonitor = new CLYNORA_ECGMonitor(canvasId);
  }
}


--- FILE: js\orb.js ---
// CLYNORA AI Voice Assistant & Glowing Interactive Orb Engine
// Immersive voice interface featuring real Web Audio API microphone analysis,
// multi-layer luminous shader simulation on Canvas, and role-aware clinical dialogue.

class CLYNORA_VoiceOrb {
  constructor(canvasId) {
    this.canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
    
    this.state = 'idle'; // 'idle' | 'listening' | 'processing' | 'speaking' | 'muted' | 'error'
    this.audioLevel = 0; // 0 to 1 normalized volume
    this.targetAudioLevel = 0;
    this.time = 0;
    this.rotation = 0;
    this.active = false;
    this.micStream = null;
    this.audioCtx = null;
    this.analyser = null;
    this.dataArray = null;

    this.resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());
  }

  resize() {
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    const rect = this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    this.size = Math.min(rect.width, rect.height) || 300;
    this.canvas.width = this.size * dpr;
    this.canvas.height = this.size * dpr;
    this.ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  }

  start() {
    this.active = true;
    this.animate();
    this.initMicrophone();
  }

  stop() {
    this.active = false;
    this.releaseMicrophone();
  }

  async initMicrophone() {
    try {
      if (navigator.mediaDevices && navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
        this.micStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
        const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        this.audioCtx = new AudioContext();
        const source = this.audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(this.micStream);
        this.analyser = this.audioCtx.createAnalyser();
        this.analyser.fftSize = 256;
        this.analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.8;
        source.connect(this.analyser);
        this.dataArray = new Uint8Array(this.analyser.frequencyBinCount);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.log("Microphone access not granted or unavailable; using simulated voice response:", err.message);
    }
  }

  releaseMicrophone() {
    if (this.micStream) {
      this.micStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      this.micStream = null;
    }
    if (this.audioCtx && this.audioCtx.state !== 'closed') {
      this.audioCtx.close().catch(() => {});
      this.audioCtx = null;
    }
  }

  setState(newState) {
    this.state = newState;
    updateOrbUIState(newState);
  }

  updateAudioSample() {
    if (this.state === 'muted' || this.state === 'error') {
      this.targetAudioLevel = 0;
    } else if (this.state === 'listening') {
      if (this.analyser && this.dataArray) {
        this.analyser.getByteFrequencyData(this.dataArray);
        let sum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < this.dataArray.length; i++) {
          sum += this.dataArray[i];
        }
        const avg = sum / this.dataArray.length;
        this.targetAudioLevel = Math.min(1.0, (avg / 128) * 1.5);
      } else {
        // Subtle ambient listening breathing
        this.targetAudioLevel = 0.2 + Math.sin(this.time * 4) * 0.15;
      }
    } else if (this.state === 'speaking') {
      // Natural speech cadence simulation
      const cad = Math.sin(this.time * 6) * Math.cos(this.time * 3);
      this.targetAudioLevel = Math.max(0.15, Math.abs(cad) * 0.85);
    } else if (this.state === 'processing') {
      this.targetAudioLevel = 0.25 + Math.sin(this.time * 8) * 0.1;
    } else {
      // Idle slow breathing
      this.targetAudioLevel = 0.08 + Math.sin(this.time * 1.8) * 0.05;
    }

    // Smooth lerp
    this.audioLevel += (this.targetAudioLevel - this.audioLevel) * 0.18;
  }

  animate() {
    if (!this.active) return;

    this.time += 0.02;
    this.updateAudioSample();

    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const w = this.size;
    const h = this.size;
    const cx = w / 2;
    const cy = h / 2;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Dynamic radius with audio expansion
    const baseRadius = (w * 0.28);
    const audioExpansion = this.audioLevel * (w * 0.14);
    const r = baseRadius + audioExpansion;

    // 1. Outer Ambient Glow Layer
    const outerGlow = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, r * 0.6, cx, cy, r * 1.6);
    if (this.state === 'muted') {
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0)');
    } else if (this.state === 'processing') {
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.4)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
    } else {
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.45)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0.4, 'rgba(45, 212, 191, 0.25)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(0.8, 'rgba(58, 91, 217, 0.15)');
      outerGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = outerGlow;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // 2. Swirling Light Vortex for Processing State
    if (this.state === 'processing') {
      this.rotation += 0.04;
    } else {
      this.rotation += 0.008;
    }

    // 3. Deformed Fluid Outer Sphere Layer
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(cx, cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.rotation);

    const fluidGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(-r, -r, r, r);
    if (this.state === 'muted') {
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(0, '#78350f');
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(1, '#d97706');
    } else {
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(0, '#0284c7');  // Deep Sky Blue
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(0.3, '#0d9488'); // Dark Teal
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(0.7, '#2563eb'); // Medical Cobalt
      fluidGradient.addColorStop(1, '#4f46e5');  // Violet
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = fluidGradient;
    ctx.beginPath();

    const points = 36;
    for (let i = 0; i <= points; i++) {
      const angle = (i / points) * Math.PI * 2;
      // Fluid harmonic wave offset
      const wave = Math.sin(angle * 4 + this.time * 3) * (this.audioLevel * 14) +
                   Math.cos(angle * 2 - this.time * 2) * (this.audioLevel * 8);
      const curR = r + wave;
      const x = Math.cos(angle) * curR;
      const y = Math.sin(angle) * curR;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();

    // 4. Luminous Inner Core Sphere
    const innerGradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(
      cx - r * 0.25, cy - r * 0.25, r * 0.1,
      cx, cy, r * 0.95
    );

    if (this.state === 'muted') {
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0, '#fde68a');
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.4, '#f59e0b');
      innerGradient.addColorStop(1, '#92400e');
    } else if (this.state === 'processing') {
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0, '#e0e7ff');
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.3, '#818cf8');
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.7, '#38bdf8');
      innerGradient.addColorStop(1, '#1e1b4b');
    } else {
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0, '#e0f2fe'); // Crisp luminous white-cyan highlight
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.2, '#38bdf8'); // Electric Cyan
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.6, '#2dd4bf'); // Radiant Teal
      innerGradient.addColorStop(0.85, '#3a5bd9'); // Medical Blue
      innerGradient.addColorStop(1, '#182245'); // Deep Navy Depth
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = innerGradient;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 0.95, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // 5. Specular Reflection Arc (Glass / Luminous Sheen)
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(cx - r * 0.28, cy - r * 0.32, r * 0.45, r * 0.22, -Math.PI / 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    const sheen = ctx.createLinearGradient(
      cx - r * 0.5, cy - r * 0.5,
      cx, cy
    );
    sheen.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)');
    sheen.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)');
    sheen.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = sheen;
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();

    requestAnimationFrame(() => this.animate());
  }
}

// Global Voice Assistant Controller
let voiceOrbInstance = null;
let voiceMuted = false;

function openVoiceMode() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('voiceOverlay');
  if (!overlay) return;

  overlay.classList.add('active');
  document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

  if (!voiceOrbInstance) {
    voiceOrbInstance = new CLYNORA_VoiceOrb('voiceOrbCanvas');
  }
  voiceOrbInstance.start();
  voiceOrbInstance.setState('idle');

  // Set greeting transcript based on user role and language
  showTranscript(getRoleGreeting());
  renderVoiceQuickPrompts();
  updateOrbLanguage();
}

function closeVoiceMode() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('voiceOverlay');
  if (!overlay) return;

  overlay.classList.remove('active');
  document.body.style.overflow = '';

  if (voiceOrbInstance) {
    voiceOrbInstance.stop();
  }
  if (window.speechSynthesis) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  }
}

function toggleVoiceMute() {
  voiceMuted = !voiceMuted;
  const btn = document.getElementById('voiceMuteBtn');
  if (voiceMuted) {
    if (voiceOrbInstance) voiceOrbInstance.setState('muted');
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.add('muted-mic');
      btn.classList.remove('active-mic');
      btn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M19 11h-1.7c0 .74-.16 1.43-.43 2.05l1.23 1.23c.56-.98.9-2.09.9-3.28zm-4.02.17c0-.06.02-.11.02-.17V5c0-1.66-1.34-3-3-3S9 3.34 9 5v.18l5.98 5.99zM4.27 3L3 4.27l6.01 6.01V11c0 1.66 1.33 3 2.99 3 .22 0 .44-.03.65-.08l1.66 1.66c-.71.33-1.5.52-2.31.52-2.76 0-5.3-2.1-5.3-5.1H5c0 3.41 2.72 6.23 6 6.72V21h2v-3.28c.91-.14 1.77-.45 2.54-.9L19.73 21 21 19.73 4.27 3z"/></svg>`;
    }
    showTranscript(t('orb_muted'));
  } else {
    if (voiceOrbInstance) voiceOrbInstance.setState('listening');
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.remove('muted-mic');
      btn.classList.add('active-mic');
      btn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 14c1.66 0 3-1.34 3-3V5c0-1.66-1.34-3-3-3S9 3.34 9 5v6c0 1.66 1.34 3 3 3zm5-3c0 2.76-2.24 5-5 5s-5-2.24-5-5H5c0 3.53 2.61 6.43 6 6.92V21h2v-3.08c3.39-.49 6-3.39 6-6.92h-2z"/></svg>`;
    }
    showTranscript(t('orb_listening'));
  }
}

function updateOrbUIState(state) {
  const pill = document.getElementById('voiceStatusPill');
  const text = document.getElementById('voiceStatusText');
  if (!pill || !text) return;

  pill.className = `voice-status-pill ${state}`;
  
  const stateLabels = {
    idle: t('orb_idle'),
    listening: t('orb_listening'),
    processing: t('orb_processing'),
    speaking: t('orb_speaking'),
    muted: t('orb_muted'),
    error: t('orb_error')
  };

  text.textContent = stateLabels[state] || state;

  // Update tester buttons highlight
  document.querySelectorAll('.state-test-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.getAttribute('data-state') === state);
  });
}

  };
  showTranscript(sampleMessages[state] || state);
}

function renderVoiceQuickPrompts() {
  const container = document.getElementById('voiceQuickPrompts');
  if (!container) return;

  const role = window.U ? window.U.role : 'doc';
  let prompts = [];

  if (role === 'doc') {
    prompts = [t('prompt_doc_1'), t('prompt_doc_2'), t('prompt_doc_3')];
  } else if (role === 'nurse') {
    prompts = [t('prompt_nurse_1'), t('prompt_nurse_2'), t('prompt_nurse_3')];
  } else if (role === 'pat') {
    prompts = [t('prompt_pat_1'), t('prompt_pat_2')];
  } else if (role === 'att') {
    prompts = [t('prompt_att_1'), t('prompt_att_2')];
  } else {
    prompts = [t('prompt_doc_1'), t('prompt_nurse_1')];
  }

  container.innerHTML = `
    <span style="width:100%;text-align:center;font-size:0.75rem;color:#94a3b8;margin-bottom:2px">
      ${t('voice_prompts_title')}
    </span>
    ${prompts.map(p => `
      <button class="voice-prompt-chip" onclick="triggerVoicePrompt('${p.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
        ${p}
      </button>
    `).join('')}
  `;
}

function updateOrbLanguage() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('voiceLangSelect');
  if (sel && typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') {
    sel.value = currentLang;
  }
  renderVoiceQuickPrompts();
  if (voiceOrbInstance) {
    updateOrbUIState(voiceOrbInstance.state);
  }
}

function showTranscript(text) {
  const box = document.getElementById('voiceTranscriptBox');
  if (!box) return;
  box.innerHTML = text;
  box.classList.add('active');
}

// Interactive prompt execution for Voice Mode
function triggerVoicePrompt(promptText) {
  if (!voiceOrbInstance) {
    voiceOrbInstance = new CLYNORA_VoiceOrb('voiceOrbCanvas');
    voiceOrbInstance.start();
  }

  // 1. User Speaks / Prompt selected
  voiceOrbInstance.setState('listening');
  const userPrefix = currentLang === 'ta' ? "à®¨à¯€à®™à¯à®•à®³à¯ à®•à¯‡à®Ÿà¯à®Ÿ à®•à¯‡à®³à¯à®µà®¿:" : currentLang === 'hi' ? "à¤†à¤ªà¤¨à¥‡ à¤ªà¥‚à¤›à¤¾:" : "You asked:";
  showTranscript(`<strong>${userPrefix}</strong> "${promptText}"`);

  // 2. Processing
  setTimeout(() => {
    voiceOrbInstance.setState('processing');
    const procMsg = currentLang === 'ta' ? "à®šà®®à¯€à®ªà®¤à¯à®¤à®¿à®¯ à®…à®³à®µà¯€à®Ÿà¯à®•à®³à¯, EWS à®®à®¤à®¿à®ªà¯à®ªà¯†à®£à¯ à®®à®±à¯à®±à¯à®®à¯ à®ªà®¤à®¿à®µà¯à®•à®³à¯ à®ªà®•à¯à®ªà¯à®ªà®¾à®¯à¯à®µà¯ à®šà¯†à®¯à¯à®¯à®ªà¯à®ªà®Ÿà¯à®•à®¿à®©à¯à®±à®©..." : currentLang === 'hi' ? "à¤¹à¤¾à¤²à¤¿à¤¯à¤¾ à¤Ÿà¥‡à¤²à¥€à¤®à¥‡à¤Ÿà¥à¤°à¥€, EWS à¤¸à¥à¤•à¥‹à¤° à¤”à¤° à¤•à¥à¤²à¤¿à¤¨à¤¿à¤•à¤² à¤°à¤¿à¤•à¥‰à¤°à¥à¤¡ à¤•à¤¾ à¤µà¤¿à¤¶à¥à¤²à¥‡à¤·à¤£..." : "Analyzing latest telemetry, EWS score, and clinical logs...";
    showTranscript(procMsg);

    // 3. Response Generation
    setTimeout(() => {
      const responseHtml = generateAIAnswer(promptText);
      voiceOrbInstance.setState('speaking');
      showTranscript(responseHtml);

      // Play vocal speech synthesis if supported
      const cleanText = responseHtml.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ');
      speakText(cleanText, () => {
        // Return to idle after speech completes
        voiceOrbInstance.setState('idle');
      });
    }, 1100);
  }, 900);
}

function speakText(text, onComplete) {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
    setTimeout(onComplete, 3500);
    return;
  }

  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  utterance.rate = 1.0;
  utterance.pitch = 1.0;

  // Language selection for voice
  if (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') {
    if (currentLang === 'ta') utterance.lang = 'ta-IN';
    else if (currentLang === 'hi') utterance.lang = 'hi-IN';
    else utterance.lang = 'en-US';
  }

  utterance.onend = onComplete;
  utterance.onerror = onComplete;
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

function transitionToTextChat() {
  closeVoiceMode();
  if (typeof toggleAssistantDrawer === 'function') {
    toggleAssistantDrawer(true);
  }
}


--- FILE: js\state.js ---
// CLYNORA State Management & Clinical Data Engine
// Preserves and upgrades original state with 5-portal data structures,
// real ESP32 Web Serial streaming, simulated live feed, and persistent storage.

const STORAGE_KEY = 'CLYNORA_STATE_V2';
const LEGACY_KEY = 'clinora.v1';

// Initial pre-seeded demo state
function getInitialState() {
  return {
    p: [],
    v: {},
    al: [],
    concerns: [],
    medReviews: [],
    devices: [],
    au: [],
    users: [],
    wards: []
  };
}

// Generate realistic 24-hour vitals curve
function generateVitalsHistory(hrStart, hrEnd, spo2Start, spo2End, tempStart, tempEnd, points) {
  const arr = [];
  const now = Date.now();
  const step = (24 * 3600000) / points;

  for (let i = points; i >= 0; i--) {
    const t = now - (i * step);
    const progress = (points - i) / points;
    
    // Add subtle organic noise
    const hrNoise = Math.sin(i * 1.5) * 2;
    const spo2Noise = (Math.cos(i * 2.1) > 0.5 ? 0 : -1);
    const tempNoise = Math.sin(i * 0.8) * 0.1;

    const hr = Math.round(hrStart + (hrEnd - hrStart) * progress + hrNoise);
    const spo2 = Math.min(100, Math.max(88, Math.round(spo2Start + (spo2End - spo2Start) * progress + spo2Noise)));
    const temp = +(tempStart + (tempEnd - tempStart) * progress + tempNoise).toFixed(1);

    arr.push({ t, k: 'hr', v: hr, s: 'device' });
    arr.push({ t, k: 'spo2', v: spo2, s: 'device' });
    arr.push({ t, k: 'temp', v: temp, s: 'device' });
  }
  return arr;
}

// Global State
var S = getInitialState();
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.S = S;

// Load persistent state
try {
  const saved = (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') ? (localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_KEY)) : null;
  if (saved) {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
    // Merge existing patients and logs
    if (parsed.p && parsed.p.length) S.p = parsed.p;
    if (parsed.v && Object.keys(parsed.v).length) S.v = parsed.v;
    if (parsed.al && parsed.al.length) S.al = parsed.al;
    if (parsed.au && parsed.au.length) S.au = parsed.au;
    if (parsed.concerns) S.concerns = parsed.concerns;
    if (parsed.medReviews) S.medReviews = parsed.medReviews;
    if (parsed.devices) S.devices = parsed.devices;
    if (parsed.users) S.users = parsed.users;
    if (parsed.wards) S.wards = parsed.wards;
  }
} catch (e) {
  console.warn("Could not load stored CLYNORA state:", e);
}

function saveState() {
  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(S));
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Error saving CLYNORA state:", e);
  }
}

// Active session variables
var U = null;       // Current authenticated user object: { id, name, role: 'doc'|'nurse'|'pat'|'att'|'admin', pid }
var activePortal = 'doc'; // Currently viewed portal: 'doc'|'nurse'|'pat'|'att'|'admin'
var view = '';      // Sub-view inside current portal
var cur = 101;      // Current active patient ID
var D = { pid: 101, last: {}, on: false, simulating: false };
var L = { ecg: [] };

if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.U = U;
  window.activePortal = activePortal;
  window.view = view;
  window.cur = cur;
  window.D = D;
  window.L = L;
}

// Logging action into audit trail
function logAction(action, category = "Clinical") {
  const entry = {
    t: Date.now(),
    u: U ? U.name : "System",
    role: U ? U.role.toUpperCase() : "SYSTEM",
    a: action,
    cat: category
  };
  S.au.unshift(entry);
  if (S.au.length > 300) S.au.pop();
  saveState();
}

// Time formatter
function tm(timestamp, format = 'short') {
  if (!timestamp) return '\u2014';
  const d = new Date(timestamp);
  if (format === 'timeOnly') {
    return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
  }
  return d.toLocaleString([], {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit'
  });
}

function timeAgo(t) {
  if (!t) return '\u2014';
  const sec = Math.floor((Date.now() - t) / 1000);
  if (sec < 60) return `${sec}s ago`;
  const min = Math.floor(sec / 60);
  if (min < 60) return `${min}m ago`;
  const hr = Math.floor(min / 60);
  if (hr < 24) return `${hr}h ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(hr / 24)}d ago`;
}

// Patient helpers
const getPatient = id => S.p.find(p => p.id === id);
const getLatestVital = (pid, key) => {
  const records = (S.v[pid] || []).filter(r => r.k === key);
  return records[records.length - 1];
};
const getVitalHistory = (pid, key, hours = 24) => {
  const cutoff = Date.now() - hours * 3600000;
  return (S.v[pid] || []).filter(r => r.k === key && r.t >= cutoff);
};

// Early Warning Score (NEWS2-inspired transparent calculation)
function calcEWS(patient) {
  if (!patient) return { rows: [], total: 0, max: 0, alert: false };
  const rows = [];
  const hr = getLatestVital(patient.id, 'hr');
  const sp = getLatestVital(patient.id, 'spo2');
  const tp = getLatestVital(patient.id, 'temp');

  if (hr) {
    let pts = 0;
    let comparison = 'General threshold';
    if (patient.base) {
      const dev = Math.abs(hr.v - patient.base) / patient.base;
      pts = dev <= 0.15 ? 0 : dev <= 0.25 ? 1 : dev <= 0.40 ? 2 : 3;
      comparison = `Compared to baseline ${patient.base} bpm (${Math.round(dev * 100)}% dev)`;
    } else {
      pts = hr.v <= 40 ? 3 : hr.v <= 50 ? 1 : hr.v <= 90 ? 0 : hr.v <= 110 ? 1 : hr.v <= 130 ? 2 : 3;
      comparison = 'Standard adult range';
    }
    rows.push({
      key: 'hr',
      label: typeof t === 'function' ? t('vitals_hr') : 'Heart Rate',
      val: `${hr.v} bpm`,
      pts,
      raw: hr.v,
      source: hr.s,
      note: comparison
    });
  }

  if (sp) {
    const pts = sp.v >= 96 ? 0 : sp.v >= 94 ? 1 : sp.v >= 92 ? 2 : 3;
    rows.push({
      key: 'spo2',
      label: typeof t === 'function' ? t('vitals_spo2') : 'SpO\u2082‚ Saturation',
      val: `${sp.v} %`,
      pts,
      raw: sp.v,
      source: sp.s,
      note: sp.v < 94 ? 'Hypoxemia warning' : 'Normal oxygenation'
    });
  }

  if (tp) {
    const pts = tp.v <= 35.0 ? 3 : tp.v <= 36.0 ? 1 : tp.v <= 38.0 ? 0 : tp.v <= 39.0 ? 1 : 2;
    rows.push({
      key: 'temp',
      label: typeof t === 'function' ? t('vitals_temp') : 'Temperature',
      val: `${tp.v} \u00B0C`,
      pts,
      raw: tp.v,
      source: tp.s,
      note: tp.v > 38.0 ? 'Elevated pyrexia' : 'Normothermia'
    });
  }

  const total = rows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.pts, 0);
  const max = Math.max(0, ...rows.map(r => r.pts));
  // Alert if aggregate score is >= 3 or any individual parameter score is 3 (severe deviation)
  const isAlert = rows.length > 0 && (total >= 3 || max === 3);

  return { rows, total, max, alert: isAlert };
}

// Ingest reading
function pushVital(pid, key, val, source = 'device') {
  if (!S.v[pid]) S.v[pid] = [];

  const recordedAt = Date.now();
  const numericValue = Number(val);

  S.v[pid].push({
    t: recordedAt,
    k: key,
    v: numericValue,
    s: source
  });

  // Maintain max 1000 readings per patient
  if (S.v[pid].length > 1000) S.v[pid].shift();

  // Persist only patients loaded from Supabase
  const patient = S.p.find(p => Number(p.id) === Number(pid));
  const supabase = window.supabaseClient;

  if (patient?.dbBacked && supabase && Number.isFinite(numericValue)) {
    supabase
      .from('vitals')
      .insert({
        patient_id: Number(pid),
        recorded_at_ms: recordedAt,
        vital_type: key,
        value: numericValue,
        source
      })
      .then(({ error }) => {
        if (error) console.error("Could not save vital to Supabase:", error);
      })
      .catch(error => {
        console.error("Vital save request failed:", error);
      });
  }
}
// Evaluate deterioration and update alert status
function evaluatePatient(patient) {
  const ews = calcEWS(patient);
  if (ews.alert && patient.status === 'checked') {
    patient.status = 'unchecked';
    patient.autoAlert = true;
    const newAlert = {
      id: Date.now(),
      pid: patient.id,
      score: ews.total,
      t: Date.now(),
      level: ews.total >= 5 ? 'critical' : 'high',
      cat: 'Deterioration Threshold',
      desc: `Automated alert triggered (Score ${ews.total}): ${ews.rows.map(r => `${r.label} ${r.val}`).join(', ')}`,
      res: null,
      assigned: patient.nurse || "On-duty Nurse"
    };
    S.al.unshift(newAlert);
    logAction(`Early Warning Alert triggered for ${patient.name} (Score ${ews.total})`, "Alert");
  } else if (!ews.alert && patient.status === 'unchecked' && patient.autoAlert) {
    patient.status = 'checked';
    patient.autoAlert = false;
    S.al.filter(a => a.pid === patient.id && !a.res).forEach(a => {
      a.res = { t: Date.now(), by: 'Automated Recovery' };
    });
    logAction(`Vitals returned to baseline/safe limits: ${patient.name}`, "Clinical");
  }
  saveState();
}

// Real-time Virtual Simulation Engine (for judges / demonstrations without physical ESP32)
let simTimer = null;
function toggleSimulation() {
  if (D.simulating) {
    clearInterval(simTimer);
    simTimer = null;
    D.simulating = false;
    logAction("Virtual ESP32 simulation stopped", "Device");
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Simulation stopped");
  } else {
    D.simulating = true;
    logAction("Virtual ESP32 simulation started for active patient", "Device");
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Virtual ESP32 streaming live data");
    
    simTimer = setInterval(() => {
      const pid = cur || (S.p[0] && S.p[0].id);
      const patient = getPatient(pid);
      if (!patient) return;
      
      const lastHR = getLatestVital(pid, 'hr')?.v || patient.base || 72;
      const lastSp = getLatestVital(pid, 'spo2')?.v || 98;
      const lastTp = getLatestVital(pid, 'temp')?.v || 36.8;

      // Realistic minor wander
      const newHR = Math.max(50, Math.min(130, Math.round(lastHR + (Math.random() - 0.49) * 2)));
      const newSp = Math.max(90, Math.min(100, Math.round(lastSp + (Math.random() > 0.8 ? (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) : 0))));
      const newTp = +(Math.max(35.5, Math.min(39.5, lastTp + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05))).toFixed(1);

      pushVital(pid, 'hr', newHR, 'device');
      pushVital(pid, 'spo2', newSp, 'device');
      pushVital(pid, 'temp', newTp, 'device');
      
      D.last.hr = Date.now();
      D.last.spo2 = Date.now();
      D.last.temp = Date.now();
      D.last.ecg = Date.now();

      evaluatePatient(patient);
      
      if (typeof updateLiveVitalsDOM === 'function') {
        updateLiveVitalsDOM();
      }
    }, 2500);
  }
  if (typeof draw === 'function') draw();
}

// Web Serial API (ESP32 connection)
let serialPort = null;
let serialReader = null;

async function connectSerial() {
  const pid = D.pid || cur;
  const patient = getPatient(pid);
  if (!patient) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Select a patient to link the device to first");
    return;
  }
  if (!('serial' in navigator)) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Web Serial requires Google Chrome or Microsoft Edge");
    return;
  }

  try {
    serialPort = await navigator.serial.requestPort();
    await serialPort.open({ baudRate: 115200 });
    D.on = true;
    logAction(`ESP32 hardware serial connected for ${patient.name}`, "Device");
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`ESP32 linked to ${patient.name}`);
    if (typeof draw === 'function') draw();

    const textDecoder = new TextDecoderStream();
    serialPort.readable.pipeTo(textDecoder.writable);
    serialReader = textDecoder.readable.getReader();
    let buffer = '';

    while (true) {
      const { value, done } = await serialReader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += value;
      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
      buffer = lines.pop();

      lines.forEach(line => {
        try {
          const json = JSON.parse(line.trim());
          ingestSerialPacket(json, patient);
        } catch (err) {
          // Incomplete packet; ignore
        }
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Web Serial connection error:", err);
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Serial connection closed or cancelled");
    D.on = false;
    if (typeof draw === 'function') draw();
  }
}

function ingestSerialPacket(data, patient) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (data.ecg !== undefined && isFinite(data.ecg)) {
    L.ecg.push(+data.ecg);
    if (L.ecg.length > 500) L.ecg.shift();
    D.last.ecg = now;
  }
  ['hr', 'spo2', 'temp'].forEach(k => {
    if (data[k] !== undefined && isFinite(data[k])) {
      pushVital(patient.id, k, data[k], 'device');
      D.last[k] = now;
    }
  });
  evaluatePatient(patient);
}




--- FILE: js\supabase-config.js ---
window.supabaseClient = window.supabase.createClient(
  "https://icckevtwwzsbrbaqlnvo.supabase.co",
  "[REDACTED_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY]"
);

let currentLang = 'en';

function setLanguage() {
  currentLang = 'en';
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.documentElement) {
    document.documentElement.lang = 'en';
  }
}

function t(key) {
  return TRANSLATIONS.en?.[key] || key;
}

if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setLanguage);
  } else {
    setLanguage();
  }
}

let currentLang = 'en';

function setLanguage() {
  currentLang = 'en';
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.documentElement) {
    document.documentElement.lang = 'en';
  }
}

function t(key) {
  return TRANSLATIONS.en?.[key] || key;
}

if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setLanguage);
  } else {
    setLanguage();
  }
}
